# Kickstarter Model naming review inventory

This is the SwiftSyntax declaration inventory for source under
`Kickstarter-iOS/2 - AppModel` at the current working tree. A row is **not** a
reviewed decision until its action, result, callers, local names and decision
columns have been completed. Re-run discovery after edits and compare counts.
Conditional compilation branches are included; target membership and macro
expansions still require separate reconciliation. Files with no function or
property declarations: `Features/Payments/AddNewCardIntent.swift`, `Features/Recommendations/SimilarProjectsState.swift`, `User Data Storage/Analytics/ThirdPartyEventInputName.swift`.
